## Addendum 79 (2026-10-03): New-Purchase sweep - "Boogie Nights, Bloody Nights" (English, slip style, 14 characters) - held by 4 detector hits, plus 5 more leaks and 32 missing headers the detectors never saw; one detector extension shipped

**Package.** Conversation `3ad97bed-3d79-4b94-9668-7da9282506b9`, package `a4e3078c-faa7-4aca-8aa3-5ab1592ad503`, USD 19.99, purchased 2026-10-03 05:50 UTC. Post-ADR-0074, post-v44. `mystery_style='character'`, `script_type='both'`, `has_accomplice=false` on the conversation although the approved concept explicitly has a killer and a helper (so accomplice branches are correct; the flag is the stale form value the completion trigger re-syncs). Held at `needs_review` from 05:58 with 4 structural defects; no ready email had been sent.

**What the standing detectors said.** `package_completion_blocking_defects()` held it for 1 `meta_text_leak` (Gio, a stray `</br>`) and 3 `dangling_quote_mark` (Lena, Roxanne, Pete). Victim mismatch, unresolved victim, role-tag leak, narration-person and the Addendum 78 relationship-matrix detector were clean. Dangling quotes after the v44 fix: 3 live hits here, so the v44 prompt fix did not fully eliminate the class (still trending down, not zero).

**Found only by the full-cast read (every non-pointform field of all 14 characters plus a programmatic scan of all columns):**
- **Leaked self-correction (detector gap):** Honey's `final_innocent` ended `Let me correct that formatting issue.` A model self-correction inside a read-aloud statement. The phrase list had "let me reconsider/reread/recalculate" but not this variant.
- **Stray backtick (detector gap):** Sandro's `reveal_confession_accomplice` ended with a lone backtick; `stray_rx` only matched a triple-backtick fence.
- **32 branch fields with no baked-in header** (accomplice branches of Billy, Chuck, Dot, Pete, Roxanne; all of Dot's innocent branches and both reveal confessions; Honey's, Billy's, Chuck's, Pete's, Roxanne's accomplice confession; Roxanne's introduction). Cosmetic in the character-access page (it strips and re-adds its own labels) but **real in the host's compiled/downloaded guide**, which concatenates the raw fields (`MysteryPackageTabView.tsx` ~L340-435), so an accomplice slip's text ran on unlabeled after the guilty text. Slip-style corpus rate for a missing accomplice header was 2.9% of characters since 2026-08-15; this package had 5 of 14.
- **2 missing pointforms:** `reveal_confession_accomplice_pointform` empty for Billy and Gio (corpus: 3 of 98 slip/both characters since 2026-09-01, two of them here). Hand-written, 7 bullets each.
- **Dusty's gender:** Dusty is gender-neutral ("they") in the customer's concept and in 12 of 14 characters. Lena wrote she/her for Dusty in ~10 places across 3 fields (plus `round2_questions`), Pete's accomplice round 4 said "Dusty herself", Francesca "a man's drink", Dot "golden girl". Normalised to they/Dusty.
- **Cross-field contradictions:** Honey's `round2_innocent` said she went to Sandro first ("That's what I did") one paragraph after saying she took it to Dusty; her `round2_guilty` said three years where her introduction says "a couple of years"; her description sentence was garbled; her rumor called Pete's coat-check instinct "a bartender's instinct". Chuck's innocent final statement hinted "a badge" (Pete's secret). Roxanne said "last night" for the same night; Sandro "behind the bar" (he is the owner); Francesca "a informant"; Pete "not short list" and a stray paragraph under ALLIES.

**Fix.** 57 fields across 11 characters, exact-match edits each required to match exactly once (`temp-files` script, removed after use; pre-fix snapshot in the session scratchpad). Applied in two phases so the auto-release could not fire on a half-fixed package: all undetected edits first, the four detector-flagged fields last. Verified live: `package_completion_blocking_defects()` NULL, all six scoped detectors 0, relationship-matrix detector NULL, no field without a header, fresh re-scan clean. `heal_completed_packages` then released it at 07:30:00 UTC and the ready email went out through the normal path (`ready_email_sent_at` 07:30:00), with the corrected content.

**Detector extension (Addendum 38 test: cheap + deterministic, severe on a read-aloud statement, so built now).** Migration `20261003120000_add_formatting_leak_and_backtick_to_meta_text_leak.sql` adds to `package_meta_text_leak()` (single source for the gate and the list): `let me (correct|fix|clean up|reformat|redo) (that|this|the|my)? ?(formatting|format)` and `formatting (issue|error|problem|mistake)`; and widens `stray_rx` from a triple-backtick fence to any backtick. Corpus: zero hits for either over all 250 packages (characters and package text) after this package's two rows were repaired, so zero false positives; synthetic tests confirm the two new patterns fire, Cyrillic and `</br>` still fire, and ordinary dialogue ("Let me correct myself, I was at the bar") does not. **Deliberately not added to the auto-remediate worker's `PROSE_LEAK_RX`**: a worker match routes to the paid `regenerate-child-content` path, a separate spend decision; a detector-only hit escalates free for a by-hand exact-match edit, the same stance as Addendum 78's stray tokens.

**Not built (considered).** A missing-header detector: cosmetic in one render path, 2.9% corpus rate, a fix that is mechanical (prepend the standard header), so left as a sweep checklist item rather than a gate that would hold paid packages. A Dusty-pronoun detector: needs the victim's gender convention per package, not cheap or deterministic.

**Left alone, on purpose.**
- Accomplice confessions that name a specific killer (Bobby names Sandro; Lena names Roxanne) while `master_context.accomplicePairings` pairs Lena's *killer* role with Roxanne as her *accomplice*, not the other way round. Same product behaviour as Addendum 78 / ADR-0125 (slip style, killer drawn at the table); not a defect in this package.
- Toni's accomplice branches point at Honey and Vera, who are Toni's paired killers. Judgement call in the model's deflection, not a factual error.
- Relationship text vs matrix: no hard contradictions (detector NULL); Chuck/Gio/Dot/Pete/Roxanne list a Neutral guest as an ally, which ADR-0134 deliberately does not flag. Child52 is not imported yet.
- Bobby's accomplice round 2 calls the skimming "a lie" while his secret and other branches admit it: a character lying in-role.

**Post-import watch.** Parent73/Child51 not exercised (slip style). Unchanged from Addendum 78.

## Addendum 80 (2026-10-03): Why "Boogie Nights" did not self-heal, and closing the gap: worker drift fixed, header and pointform classes added (free + approved paid)

**Question that prompted this (Jonathan):** which of the 57 hand-fixed fields were things the system should heal by itself, and which needed new detectors, given the goal that no package is swept by hand. That goal is now written into the North Star ("Operating Principle: Autonomous Quality"): prevent at source, detect, heal deterministically, escalate only judgment.

**Finding 1: the existing self-heal was stalled by a paired-predicate drift introduced the same morning.** `auto_remediation_log` for the package shows `dangling_quote_mark` attempted at 06:13 and 06:43: the worker stripped the two single-quote fields, re-detected, saw Pete's trailing `”` still flagged (Addendum 78 had widened the DETECTOR to double quotes at 10:00 the day before while the worker's `DANGLING_QUOTE_RX` still only knew `'` and `’`), reverted all of it, and at 07:13 hit the 2-attempt cap (`skip:attempt_cap`). `template_artifact` for Gio's `</br>` escalated `no_mechanical_fix` (no strip existed). So a customer-held package waited for a human on two classes that are 100% mechanical. Lesson (third occurrence of this bug shape): a detector change and its heal change ship in the same commit, and the heal's patterns live in ONE shared module.

**Fix.** New dependency-free module `supabase/functions/auto-remediate-packages/glitch-strip.ts` (+ unit tests `glitch-strip.test.mjs`, run with `node --experimental-strip-types`): all four quote glyphs for the dangling-quote strip; free removal of stray `</br>` (to a paragraph break), closing tags, backticks and a whole-line leaked formatting self-correction ("Let me correct that formatting issue.", length-capped, so dialogue like "Let me correct myself" is never touched). Replaying the new logic on the package's pre-fix text reproduces all 6 hand edits exactly. Deployed (v19+), `verify_jwt` unchanged.

**New class `missing_branch_header` (free).** Migration `20261003130000` adds `package_missing_branch_header()` / `list_packages_with_missing_branch_header()` (slip style only: in detective style 57% of round scripts are headerless by design). Heal = copy the header from the same field on the other characters in the same package (majority among header-bearing siblings, strict majority, ties refuse), so it is in the right language and role wording with no translation. Replay on the pre-fix package: 32 of 32 fields derive the same header I wrote by hand. First live run healed 9 delivered packages (≈90 fields) and correctly escalated one where the Spanish reveal headers differed (MI vs TU); that exposed a bug in my first majority rule (headerless siblings were counted as votes) and it was fixed and re-run, 13 more fields healed.

**New class `empty_pointform` (paid, approved by Jonathan 2026-10-03: "yes").** `package_empty_pointform()` / list: prose longer than 200 chars whose `*_pointform` sibling is empty, only when the conversation's `script_type` is `both` or `pointForm`, `accusations` excluded (empty by design for innocents). Heal reuses the existing `generate-pointform-summaries` Sonnet path at the existing `POINTFORM_REGEN_COST_USD = 0.05` per character, own attempt cap, under the existing $10/day cap. Corpus since 2026-05: roughly 1-9 packages a month with 1-2 affected characters. First live run: 5 packages, $0.30.

**Gate.** Migration `20261003130100` adds both classes to `package_completion_blocking_defects()` (built from the live function text; one block added). Only transitions into `completed` and the heal/promote crons call the gate, so delivered packages are not re-flagged. State after the first runs: all four classes (headers, pointform, dangling quote, meta leak) at 0 across the last 30 days; the only delivered package with a blocking defect is the known confirmed false positive on "Blood On The Mead-bench".

**Still manual (the roadmap to zero).** (1) Victim pronoun drift: prompt-level fix in progress (Child53). (2) Cross-field contradictions, wrong names/amounts/ownership, typos: judgment; an LLM review pass is proposed separately (ADR-0135, Proposed) and is NOT built; no paid judge runs without explicit approval. (3) Evidence/image defects etc. unchanged.

## Addendum 81 (2026-10-03): New-Purchase sweep - "Hedberg Hollow" (EN, slip style, 16 characters, accomplice) and "The Great S'more Heist Of Camp Sacajawea" (EN, detective, 10 characters) - first two live purchases after Child55; the self-heal ran but a heal-written confession and a recurring "confession names a specific helper" class got through; regenerate-child-content timeouts fixed

**Context.** Two purchases 61 seconds apart (20:25 and 20:26 UTC). Jonathan's hypothesis on seeing them: "they both had a chance to self heal". Checked in `auto_remediation_log` first: **both did.** S'more got one free `strip_dangling_quote:final_statement` and completed at 20:44 (email sent same minute). Hedberg was held at 20:36 (`missing_round_content` x2 and `missing_role_branch_content`: Aaron had NO guilty-branch scripts, Tommy had NO innocent-branch scripts), and the heal worked four times and then stopped: 20:40:15 `delegate:regenerate_child_content|invoke_failed`, 20:40:41 regen of Tommy's `final_accomplice` (0.10 USD), a quote strip, 11 free branch headers, then 20:43:11 `skip:attempt_cap` -> escalated. So the heal covered the small things, was blocked by an infrastructure failure on the one that mattered, and one of its outputs was itself a defect (below).

**Finding 1 (infrastructure): `regenerate-child-content` hit the database statement timeout.** The function's re-detect gate calls four `list_packages_with_*` detectors with `_since = 2000-01-01` (a full-history scan) before and after writing. As the corpus grew this exceeds the timeout. Live: 5 of my 8 manual attempts died in the pre-check (about 9 s), the worker's first call failed `invoke_failed`, and one call (Aaron, 40 s) **wrote the new content and then died in the post-write gate**: the outer `catch` returned 500, left the unverified content in place and logged no spend row (I added the ledger rows by hand: 0.20 + 0.10). Fix (deployed by CLI, `verify_jwt` still true, 401 confirmed): detectors are scoped to one day before the package's own `created_at` (same package, a fraction of the rows; falls back to full history if the lookup fails), and a gate that cannot run now reverts the writes and logs the spend (`gate_unavailable|reverted`, outcome failed) instead of throwing. Six free dry runs after deploy: 0.6 to 2.2 s, 6 of 6.

**Finding 2 (heal output): the regen prompt invited a confession in the Final Statement.** The `finalAccomplice` schema line read "reveal accomplice role only at the very end if at all". The heal regenerated Tommy Besaw's `final_accomplice` as a full accomplice confession ("Someone came to me not long after we found Shane... I helped put something out of sight... I chose them over the truth"), read BEFORE the reveal (the detective script says the final statements are denials). `list_packages_with_final_statement_confession_leak` did not fire because its accomplice regex only matched "I helped (him|her|them)". Fixes: the schema line and footer now say the accomplice stays in denial in every pre-reveal field; the detector regex is widened (migration `20261003210000`: helped hide/bury/cover/put/move/get rid, hid, chose X over, covered for) and the negation list gains neither/nor/never, which also removes the one false positive the old regex produced (Shannon Barrett, "neither one of them means I killed him"). Corpus check before applying: 0 hits since 2026-08-06 on the new regex. The function is still list-only, not part of `package_completion_blocking_defects()`.

**Finding 3 (new recurring class, NOT fixed in the corpus): a slip-style confession names a specific helper or culprit.** In a slip game the murderer and accomplice slips are drawn at the table, so a confession must not name another cast member. Five confessions in Hedberg did: Elizabeth (guilty) "Fabian found me", Holly Beth (guilty) "Jim found me", Joe (guilty) "Shannon found me", Jacki (accomplice) "looking for Aaron and found him", Jim (accomplice) "When Holly Beth came to me"; Elizabeth's accomplice confession also assumed the culprit is male (him/his) and invented a shovel. All genericised by exact-match edit (and the pointform bullets). **The same shape exists elsewhere**: a raw corpus query since 2026-04-01 gives 79 hits in 11 packages (noisy: first-name extraction produced false hits such as "saw"), with confirmed real cases in Boogie Nights ("Sandro came to me that night", "Roxanne came to me backstage") and Champagne & Crocodile Tears ("Blair came to me in a panic weeks ago"). The regen prompt now forbids it (both confession fields, both directions, he/she too). **Open: detector + heal + Make Child56 prompt + backlog sweep, see decisions below.** The LLM reviewer is blind to it: `review_performance` for style character, category secret_leak: 0 real finds, 6 misses.

**S'more (detective): Child51 (innocents must not reference the murderer) did not hold.** Chef Gus named Daisy as the borrower of the flour sacks in her relationships ("I had no idea what she was really planning when I lent her those flour sacks"), her secret and her final statement ("never once thinking twice about why a ten-year-old wanted to spend so much time lingering by my flour sacks"), contradicting `master_context` (Gus cannot recall which camper). Marcy's final statement echoed the culprit's motive ("wanting to be seen doesn't excuse taking something"). Pete's R4 question was addressed to Camp Director Hazel, an NPC who is not one of the 10 players. The game overview said "one small camper's quiet resentment holds the real answer". The reviewer (run by the cron at 21:04, before any edit, so recall is valid) found the Gus relationships leak and the overview, but only that one of Gus's three fields.

**Reviewer vs hand sweep (both swept in full; Hedberg's reviewer ran by hand after the heal and one manual matrix edit but before the sweep edits, so heal-fixed items are excluded).**

| | findings | real | debatable | false | my misses (not in findings) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Hedberg (slip, 16) | 19 (1 high) | 18 | 1 | 0 | 8: the Tommy final_accomplice confession, 5 helper/culprit-naming confessions, Elizabeth him/shovel, Tommy "behind his own garage" |
| S'more (detective, 10) | 12 (1 high) | 10 | 2 | 0 | 5: Gus secret + final (same leak), Marcy motive echo, Pete question to a non-cast NPC, Daisy volunteering the sacks (low, not fixed) |

The reviewer found about 13 things I had not (time-reference slips "buried a husband", "since the funeral", "days ago", "all week", "last night", "the night he died"; Hawk "two decades" vs fourteen years; Gus "kitchen full of missing marshmallows" and "one vanished crate"; Sheriff "pick a lock that's been forced"; Tommy "how close" vs his own secret). Cost: 0.79 USD (Hedberg, by hand) and 0.40 USD (S'more, cron). My own false positive: Dan's "B O P" is Shane's nickname (the materials list uses it), not a garble; reworded to "Shane", harmless.

**Post-import watch (Child53/54/55, Child51/52, Parent73), per item, as CLAUDE.md asked:**
- (a) Language pack: both English, so register is not exercised; canonical headers did NOT fully hold: the heal prepended 11 missing branch headers to Hedberg. (Cannot tell from the DB whether v55 or an older Child produced that run; check the Make execution history.)
- (b) Victim pronouns: S'more has no victim; Hedberg's victim is named and male, consistent. No drift, but not exercised.
- (c) Chained context: same-character contradictions still appear: Hawk two decades/fourteen years, Marcy twelve/eleven (twice), Tommy relationships vs secret.
- (d) Relationships follow the matrix: one contradiction (Tommy lists Heather as a rival, matrix Friendly) fixed by hand; Heather's own text ("nothing outright hostile" about Ricky, matrix Hostile) is softer than the matrix and was left. Child52 partly holds.
- Parent73: Hedberg's arrest line is generic, no names, no self-correction: held. Child51: failed (above).
- Finding counts vs baselines: S'more 12 (baseline about 20 per detective), Hedberg 19 on 16 characters (baseline about 13).

**Customer was editing live.** S'more's characters were being renamed in the editor while I worked (names changed between 20:47 and 21:09, e.g. "Ranger Petre "); my first two edits missed because they matched by name. All edits are substring replacements (no whole-field overwrites), so they do not clobber the customer's edits. Hedberg was released and emailed at 21:00:00 by the cron, before my fixes landed; the fixes are live on the same package.

**Decisions made.** Paid regen spend approved by Jonathan: 0.30 USD for three calls, then 0.10 more for Tommy's questions (I had scoped the first request to the scripts only). Reviewer by hand on Hedberg (0.79, pre-approved in CLAUDE.md). Not fixed: S'more's Round 2 evidence card (flour sacks) is a Round 3/4 clue and the card has a Flux image, so fixing it needs a new card plus a new image; Poppy's question about Willow's hand-feeding is left because that "secret" is known to seven characters.

**Open decisions for Jonathan.** (1) Build the "confession names another cast member" detector (needs careful name extraction, the raw query is noisy), a heal (regen of the reveal confessions, about 0.10 USD per character) and the Make Child56 prompt rule, and sweep the delivered packages that have it (at least 3 more). (2) Add the confession-leak check to the completion gate (then it needs a heal path). (3) Reviewer prompt: add the helper-naming rule and tell it to check `secret` and `final_*` for the same leak when it flags `relationships`. (4) The S'more evidence-card ordering.

## Addendum 82 (2026-10-04): the "confession names another cast member" class is now detected, gated, healed and prevented; the pointform step turned out to be the weak link

**Decision (Jonathan, 2026-10-04): "yes" to the open decision in Addendum 81** (detector + heal + Child56 prompt rule + backlog sweep). Built in the order that never holds a package without a heal path: detector, regen gate, worker, then the completion gate.

**Detector.** Migration `20261004100000`: `package_confession_names_cast_member(pkg)` and `list_packages_with_confession_names_cast_member(since)` (one row per character with the flagged `reveal_confession_*` fields). Case-sensitive match on name tokens from OTHER characters (dual names give both, a surname the speaker shares is skipped, titles are a stop-list); guilty confession: "NAME found/helped/took/told/... me|my|us"; accomplice confession: "NAME came to me", "protected/protecting/covered for/helped NAME", "NAME was standing/dropped/swung/did it", "found NAME standing/shaking/...". Deliberately NOT matched: bare "found NAME" and "protecting NAME" in the guilty branch (a spouse found at the party, "I told myself I was protecting Joe" are legitimate; a first draft flagged Joe's "found Shannon"). Skipped when the package has a predetermined murderer/accomplice role (naming is legitimate there). A substring pre-filter took a 30-character cast from seconds to 0.15 s. Corpus result since 2026-04-01: 12 packages, 53 characters (all slip style, none with a predetermined murderer), every hit read by hand; Hedberg and S'more are clean.

**Gate and heal.** Migration `20261004100100` adds the class to `package_completion_blocking_defects()` by patching the live function text. `auto-remediate-packages` gets a handler that groups all flagged characters of a package into one `regenerate-child-content` call (hint `confession_names_cast_member`, only the flagged fields), and `regenerate-child-content` now includes the new detector in its own before/after gate and regression guard (so a regen that still names someone, or names someone new, is reverted). The validation trigger skips anything already `completed` and the heal cron only promotes from `needs_review`, so delivered packages are never re-held by this; only new slip packages with the defect are held, healed and released.

**Test on a delivered package (Boogie Nights, 2 characters, 0.20 USD).** Regen fixed both accomplice confessions (generic "someone came to me", first person), detector clear. **The follow-up pointform refresh then wrote FRENCH bullets for Lena Lush's two confessions** (English package): the known "French bullets on English prose" class (4 earlier cases) in `generate-pointform-summaries`' separate reveal call. Hand-wrote English bullets. Source fix, deployed: the function now measures an English stopword ratio on the source prose, tells the model explicitly "the source is ENGLISH, write English" and retries once if any returned bullet block is not English (non-English sources unchanged). Offline check: French bullets score 0.00, English 0.2+, thresholds 0.07 prose / 0.04 bullets. Not yet seen live on a fresh generation.

**Prevention (Make).** `temp-files/build-child-v56.py` writes `MM Live - Child (Unified)56-NoNamedHelper.blueprint.json` (local, gitignored like v50 to v55): two exact-match edits in the output_schema of node 513 (`revealConfessionGuilty`) and 517 (`revealConfessionAccomplice`, whose line asked for "their relationship to the murderer"): never name another character as accomplice, murderer or helper, no he/she, use "someone / the person I was protecting / they". The Make `finalAccomplice` prompt already forbade admissions, so Tommy's leak was only the regen prompt (Addendum 81). **Child56 is built but not imported; Jonathan imports it. Number 56 is claimed here: do not reuse it.** Until it is imported, every new slip package with an accomplice is expected to be held by this class and healed (about 0.10 USD per flagged character plus 0.05 for pointform), which is the cost of not having prevention yet.

**Backlog (NOT run, awaiting a decision): the real size is larger than the "at least 3" in Addendum 81.** 12 delivered packages, 53 characters. Last 30 days (Sep 4 onward): The Final Cut 3, The Gods Must Be Gossiping 8, The Multiverse Gala 10, Dead On Arrival 3, Champagne & Crocodile Tears 7, Ghost In The Uplink 4 = 35 characters, about 3.50 USD regen + 1.75 pointform = 5.25 USD (the shared daily cap is 5.00, so it spans two days or needs the cap raised). Older (Aug to Sep 1): Operation Thirty 7, Elementary 4, Love Island 2, Muerte En El Micrófono 1, Casa Ferrel 1 = 15 characters, about 2.25 USD more. Recommendation: the 30-day window only (parties for older packages are over).

**Key files (Addendum 82).** `supabase/migrations/20261004100000_add_confession_names_cast_member_detector.sql`, `20261004100100_add_confession_names_cast_member_to_completion_gate.sql`, `supabase/functions/auto-remediate-packages/index.ts`, `supabase/functions/regenerate-child-content/index.ts`, `supabase/functions/generate-pointform-summaries/index.ts`, `temp-files/build-child-v56.py` (local).

**Backlog result (2026-10-04, Jonathan: "any way we can do that manually vs spending with API?").** Done by hand in the session, zero Anthropic API spend: 43 confession fields rewritten in 7 delivered packages (The Final Cut 3, Dead On Arrival 3, Ghost In The Uplink 4, Elementary 3, Champagne & Crocodile Tears 9, The Gods Must Be Gossiping 8, The Multiverse Gala 12 fields counting guilty and accomplice, plus Boogie Nights done earlier with the paid heal). Each rewrite keeps the speaker's own actions and removes the named helper or culprit, the culprit-specific backstory (hidden marriage, shell venture, "spectral arms", and similar details that only fit one suspect) and he/she for the other person; for the 4 `pointForm` packages (customer sees only the bullets) the pointform bullets were rewritten too. Applied by REST with a dry run that asserted every anchor exactly once, before-values saved for rollback. Result: the detector returns 0 rows for packages since 2026-09-01. One row, Elementary's Dr. Jamie Whatsit ("protected Bones", a years-old habit), was a detector false positive: left untouched and the accomplice pattern now carries a `(?!\s+from)` lookahead. 11 character rows remain in four August packages (Operation Thirty 7, Love Island 2, Muerte En El Micrófono 1, Casa Ferrel 1), not touched (parties over); the same by-hand route is available.

## Addendum 83 (2026-10-04): New-Purchase sweep - "Murder By Copy" (EN, slip style, 5 characters, no accomplice) - first package through the review-before-release pipeline; the reviewer scored 5 of 13; the leaked `[CLOSING:` placeholder is now detected, healed and prevented

**Context.** The order sat unstarted for 7 hours (ADR-0139), was started by hand, restarted once through the fixed trigger, then ran the whole chain by itself: generation 08:00 to 08:08 UTC, gate held it (Amber missing round content, Sheila 4 missing branch headers), free header heal 08:13, Amber re-fired 08:20, review 08:22 to 08:23, release and ready email 08:23:32 (ADR-0138 Addendum 1). The reviewer's 5 findings were all real and applied before any hand edit, so the recall measurement is valid. Full-cast read of all 5 characters, the detective script, the evidence cards and `master_context`.

**What the reviewer found (5, all real, all auto-applied):** "a week later" in a one-night game, a garbled brace line, "an humiliating", "months" against weeks, Joe "transferred in from Flin Flon" against the introduction.

**What nothing caught (8 logged in `package_review_misses`):**
1. **Hard: `[CLOSING: 1 short paragraph where the detective closes the scene.]` printed into the detective script.** Four packages carry it (2026-07-24, 07-27, 10-01, 10-04), always the same slot. Cause: the Parent prompt's own closing slot is written as exactly that bracketed directive in all 4 routes, while its hygiene rule forbids bracketed placeholders, so template and rule disagree. `package_meta_text_leak` matched `[closing paragraph` but not `[closing:`.
2. **Hard: two more Flin Flon facts** (detective opening "formerly of the Flin Flon office", Amber background "joined the Northern Team from Flin Flon"); `master_context` and three characters say Joe works out of Flin Flon. The reviewer fixed the same fact in Darion's background and missed these (nondeterministic recall on one fact).
3. **Hard: Sheila's final statement and its pointform cite a "red trace near the copier that points at my van"** that no card, script or other character mentions (it exists only in the concept's evidence list).
4. **Debatable, corpus norm:** Sheila's relationships name Darion (Neutral in the matrix) as her rival and omit Shawna (Hostile); Shawna's secret and relationships are third person while her four peers are first person; Darion's and Brenda's final statements volunteer the secret their own fields say to protect; three of five secrets carry no baked-in `## YOUR SECRET` header. Corpus checks: 28 percent of characters (887 across 79 packages) list a Neutral pair as a rival and 28 percent omit a Hostile partner; about 55 English packages in 60 days have third-person secrets; 22 percent of 696 characters lack the secret header. None of these is a clean defect class, so none was gated.

**Fixes applied by hand to this package:** placeholder removed, both Flin Flon facts, Shawna's secret and relationships rewritten in first person with the header, Sheila's relationships and the dangling clue (and the pointform bullets that quoted it), headers added to the three bare secrets. Originals are in the session scratchpad.

**Detect, heal, prevent (the cheap deterministic class only, per the Addendum 38 rule):**
- **Detect and gate:** migration `20261004140000_meta_text_leak_closing_label.sql` adds `\[closing:` to `package_meta_text_leak` (patched in place from the live function). It flags the three older packages; the gate and `list_packages_with_meta_text_leak` use it, so a new package with the label is held.
- **Heal (free):** `auto-remediate-packages` got the paired `ARTIFACT_SPAN_RX` and `ARTIFACT_TOKEN_RX` entries, deployed. The standalone line is dropped, an embedded one escalates. The three older delivered packages (A Feast For The Dying, The Masked Betrayal, Death By Dessert) were stripped by hand with the same rule; each still has its real closing paragraph.
- **Prevent:** `temp-files/build-parent-v74.py` writes `MM Live - Parent74 (Closing Slot Not Bracketed).blueprint.json` (local, gitignored): the four bracketed `[CLOSING: ...]` slots become a plain instruction with no label or brackets ("write it here as plain narration, with no label, no brackets"). Exact-match regex, asserted once per route; diff against Parent73 is those four nodes only. **Not imported until Jonathan does it.**
- Still flagged by the same detector: one older package, "Death By High Tea", not looked at yet.

**Decisions and what was deliberately not done:**
- The reviewer prompt was NOT widened. Its IGNORE list already excludes matrix, header and style items on the grounds that other tools own them; the corpus checks show those are norms, and precision (100 percent here) matters more than recall for auto-apply. The one real recall lever left is nondeterminism on a repeated fact (the Flin Flon pair); the idea is to propagate an applied fact fix by searching every other field for the same entity, noted for later, not built.
- The relationship-matrix detector was NOT widened (28 percent of characters would fire).
- The `Amber` "escalated" log row is by design: `notify-generation-issue` re-fires the Make child scenario fire-and-forget and logs `escalated` with a flat 0.15 USD, pending confirmation; the gate cleared two minutes later.

**Measurement.** Recall 56 percent counting hard misses only (5 of 9), 38 percent counting everything (5 of 13); precision 100 percent. Earlier pilots quoted 70 to 80 percent; a single package is a small sample, but the misses are in classes the prompt tells it to ignore or that the detectors own. Scoreboard row added. `review_performance` after this sweep: single-generation slips 100 percent recall (slip 12 of 12, detective 3 of 3), wrong_fact 67 to 73 percent, cross_field_contradiction 50 percent.

**Key files.** `supabase/migrations/20261004140000_meta_text_leak_closing_label.sql`, `supabase/functions/auto-remediate-packages/index.ts`, `temp-files/build-parent-v74.py` (local), `docs/autonomy-scoreboard.md`.

## Addendum 84 (2026-10-06): New-Purchase sweep - "The Roslyn's Clay And The Cracked Foundations" (EN, slip style, 6 characters, no accomplice) and "El Último Trago" (ES, detective style, 21 characters, accomplice) - a slip reveal that named the culprit, a degenerate "until until until" loop the loop detector had missed, and a 42K-character customer brief that never reached generation

**Context.** Two held packages (`needs_review`) and two customer messages from the same person (El Último Trago). The reviewer's cron ran on Roslyn's Clay AFTER my fixes (released 06:23 UTC, ready email sent), so this is not a valid recall measurement and no `package_review_sweeps` row was written.

### Roslyn's Clay (slip, 6 characters)
**Held by:** self-directed questions (Bridie, Martin) that the worker's retarget heal tried twice and reverted. The 12 missing branch headers had already been healed for free.

**Found by reading the full cast (all fixed, all verified):**
1. **The detective's reveal named the culprit.** "Martin. It's time you told this room the truth ... Martin, I am arresting you ... Take him away", plus a motive sentence only Martin fits. In a slip game the culprit is drawn at game time, and Martin was the customer. 1 of the 50 slip packages with a reveal section (a second, older one, "The Workshop Of St. Nick" 2026-08-31, names both murderer and accomplice, see below). Rewritten generically (the form every other slip package uses).
2. **Bridie spread a rumor about herself** (and her pointform said "imply this is about yourself"). Replaced with a rumor about Rick, text and pointform.
3. **Martin's third rumor had a garbled header** ("About Bridie's dealings being watched, not Bridie herself") and no target. Retargeted to Ness, text and pointform.
4. **Bridie's Round 3 question 3** was headed "To Bridie's own household aside - To Lauren"; **Martin's Round 3 question 3** was addressed to himself and empty. Both fixed (this is what held the gate).
5. A garbled "I'm only ever been the first" (Lauren, final innocent), and a stray blank paragraph (Ness, Round 3).

**The customer's explicit requests that never reached the package** (short user messages before the concept, and the concept's own write-up dropped them): the "riding about town like mad hamsters" family joke (added by hand to Lauren's introduction, text and pointform), Captain Smith / the Titanic gag, and most of the fringe cast (George, Jazz, Don Bur, Wayne Walker, Nello, Ted Smith; Roy, Mark, Taylor, Williams and Shovellin do appear), plus most of the dialect words (ar, mardy, nesh, bostin, conna, shunna, asthee). The hamster line was added first; after Jonathan's go-ahead the cast and dialect were woven back in as one sentence each in five introductions plus point-form bullets, and Nello in the detective's opening (facts only from the approved concept).

**Reviewer interaction.** The reviewer's auto-apply then REWROTE the hamster phrase ("forever driving about town with that cart of his", text and pointform) as a "garbled, anachronistic simile". It cannot know the phrase was requested. Restored by hand, finding marked `false`. Its other two findings were right (a garbled "found dead set against a man now found dead" sentence, applied; Ness's final statement hinting at her own secret, applied by hand). **Open:** the reviewer needs the customer's verbatim requests (or an exemption for phrases the customer asked for); see decisions.

**Not fixed, deliberately:** two shared holes that come from the generator itself (`master_context` hedges them: "tied to the cottages or cart disputes"): Ness and Rick's "fresh wrong" is never defined in any field, six scripts ask about it; Bridie's secret is never stated, only "something dark about Teapot". Both are playable by improvisation; inventing a canonical answer would be a creative decision.

### El Último Trago (detective, 21 characters, Spanish)
**Not repairable by a sweep.** Findings, all verified:
1. **The customer's canon never reached generation** (ADR-0144): `user_conversation` was 8,504 characters (the last 5 chat turns) against a 42,225-character canon. None of the 500/100/400 fraud, the funeral-parlour front, the false confession, the orphanage son or the somnífero appears anywhere; the Libro Negro is a plain ledger card. Verified by search of every field.
2. **The pipeline cannot build the structure he specified** even with the canon: one victim, one murderer plus an optional accomplice, fixed rounds, no ghost phase. Juanpa and Consuelo were generated as ordinary playable suspects ("redHerring") while the detective announces Juanpa dead; Consuelo is never murdered. The package also adds an external "Inspector Medianoche" against "no external detectives".
3. **The customer's Golden Rule is broken in the shipped evidence:** the reveal says "todo apunta a una sola persona ... sus propios meseros la vieron donde juró no haber estado ... una marca de labial", i.e. a single clue and a witness identifying Paulina. The canon forbids exactly that.
4. **A degenerate generation loop truncated 6 fields on 4 characters** ("until until until ..." in Niurka's Round 2/3/4 scripts, Michelle's secret and final statement, Verónica's final statement, one stray "until" in Morgana's Round 2). Verónica (the accomplice) also has NO point-form at all (the pointform heal failed twice, "apply_failed"). The meta-text detector flagged 3 characters and MISSED Verónica: its "English `until` in foreign prose" rule exempts any field that contains "the/and/with/that", and the loop itself contains "the".
5. `role_tag_leak` on Remedios is the known false-positive shape (a nickname parenthetical, "Verónica ... ('La Dama de Obsidiana')").

**Held, not touched.** No repair was made: regenerating the four looped characters costs about 0.60 USD (0.15 each, paid) and any repair of a package that cannot meet the brief is wasted if the order is regenerated or refunded. The decision (refund, regenerate with the canon, or both) is Jonathan's; see the hand-over.

### Detect, heal, prevent
- **Detect and gate (shipped, migration `20261006100000_slip_reveal_names_culprit_and_word_loop_detectors.sql`, applied live):**
  - `package_slip_reveal_names_cast_member()` + `list_packages_with_slip_reveal_names_cast_member()` + a gate block (`slip_reveal_names_culprit.<name>`). Looks only at the last `## ` section of a slip package's detective script (the header is translated, so it is found by position), excludes the victim's name tokens and title words, and counts only culprit-ADDRESSING shapes (an accusation or arrest word near the name, "<name> is the killer / under arrest / you are", the name as a direct address). The first version flagged any mention and hit 8 of 131 slip packages, 6 of them clue references ("Mint Chip's supply network"); the tightened version flags exactly: Roslyn's pre-fix (verified by rebuilding its old text), "The Workshop Of St. Nick" (real) and "Death At The Deadwood Saloon" (an April legacy fixed-murderer vote-card design, a known false positive). English plus the main verbs of es/fr/de. **Detection and escalation only**, same tier as `reveal_name_fusion`: a hand edit takes a minute and a template heal would lose the evidence-specific reveal.
  - `package_meta_text_leak()` now also flags five or more identical consecutive words (`\m(\w{2,})\M(\s+\1\M){4,}`), so Verónica is caught. Corpus check first: 4 hits, all on this package, none anywhere else. Both existing functions were patched by exact-text replacement on the live definition with asserts, not retyped.
  - The gate still shows exactly two packages with defects: El Último Trago and the known false positive "Blood On The Mead-bench".
- **Prevent (code, committed, not deployed):** ADR-0144, customer-written briefs before the approved concept now reach the Parent.
- **Prevent (built; imported into Make 2026-10-06, not yet seen on a real purchase): Parent76.** Cause found: a prompt contradiction in the two slip-style script calls (routes 1 and 3, flow 9). The accomplice beat says "NO FIXED CULPRIT", but the reveal says "naming the actual murderer/culprit" and the closing paragraph (copied from the fixed-culprit routes by Parent73's NAME RULE) says the detective "names both ... by their own name exactly as established in the master_context". 129 of 131 slip packages came out generic because master_context names no one; 2 did not. `temp-files/build-parent-v76.py` writes `MM Live - Parent76 (Slip Reveal Names No One).blueprint.json` (local, gitignored): the reveal and closing now say the culprit is decided at the table, name no one, and use "the guilty player" and they/them. Exact-match replacement asserted once per call; the fixed-culprit routes are untouched (diff: the two slip calls only). Judge it on the next slip purchase with the new detector (`slip_reveal_names_culprit` should stay empty). Next blueprint numbers: Parent77, Child58.
- **Reviewer customer-wording guard (shipped, deployed `review-package-quality` v9):** auto-apply now skips any finding whose quote contains a run of 5 or more consecutive words that the customer typed in the chat (`quoteIsCustomerWording` in `review-core.ts`, 6 offline checks, free and deterministic); the finding stays open with a note for a human. Closes the "mad hamsters" overwrite found on Roslyn's Clay. Not covered: a customer request that only paraphrases (the guard is verbatim by design).

### Decisions and what was deliberately not done
- No LLM-judge tool, as before; every finding above came from reading.
- "The Workshop Of St. Nick" (delivered 2026-08-31, named murderer and accomplice in the closing reveal): first left alone as probably already played, then repaired on Jonathan's go-ahead (reveal rewritten without names; detector and gate clean).
- The reviewer's customer-request blindness was closed with the verbatim guard above rather than by sending the chat to the paid reviewer prompt (free, deterministic; a paraphrased request is still not protected).
- The structural limit (one victim, no ghost phase) is a product fact, not a defect to patch; whether to say so on the concept page is a decision for Jonathan.

**Key files.** `supabase/migrations/20261006100000_slip_reveal_names_culprit_and_word_loop_detectors.sql`, `supabase/functions/_shared/conversation-briefs.ts`, `supabase/functions/mystery-webhook-trigger/index.ts`, `docs/adr/0144-customer-written-briefs-before-the-approved-concept-reach-generation.md`.


## Addendum 85 (2026-10-06): New-Purchase sweep - "The Vellacourt Gala: A Killing Among Masterpieces" (EN, detective style, 14 then 13 characters, accomplice) - the approved cast was replaced by a stale draft's; stacked roster annotations fixed; a character with no introduction or secret now holds the gate

**Trigger.** Purchase notification plus a contact-form message from the customer: the character count was out of date and the page sat on "Final quality checks".

**What was found.**
1. The delivered package had 14 sheets built from an older draft (Lesedi's Friend #1, Lesedi's Friend #3, Ellis/Elise) and no Shreya or Kuei sheet, although her final approved roster (`approved_concept_message_id` 8cde62d0, 12:00 UTC) had 13 names including both. `master_context` and 11 scripts used the right cast, so they named two people without a sheet.
2. William had `introduction` NULL and `secret` empty. No detector, no gate and the reviewer all missed it.
3. "Stuck" was a stale tab: the package was released and the ready email sent at 12:16:17, the browser's last package poll was 12:08:51.

**Root cause (edge logs 12:04:30-35).** Roster lines 11-13 had a gender tag and a role tag (`**William** (M) *(safe-cut)* - ...`); the roster regexes allowed one annotation, so 10 of 13 parsed. 10 < `player_count` 14 - 2 triggered `extractCharactersWithClaude`, which scanned the chat and returned 14 names from the 11:32 draft; the upgrade branch accepted it because it was longer. Same family as ADR-0069 (a stale cast winning over the approved one), different mechanism.

**Fix.** Both regexes take any number of annotation groups (`*` instead of `?`). The fallback upgrade is accepted only when it contains every name the approved message already yielded (`keepsApprovedCast`), so it can add names but never replace the approved cast. Tests in `scripts/__tests__/conceptSnapshot.test.mjs` (the stacked-annotation check fails on the old code). Deployed via `scripts/deploy-roster-functions.sh`.

**Repair.** Re-fired generation through the service-role path on the existing package (no second ready email, because `ready_email_sent_at` was already set; that also means the review-before-release pipeline did not hold it, so the reviewer was run by hand, 0.47 USD). Extraction logged `method=regex, count=13`, `player_count` synced to 13, the new package has exactly the customer's 13 names, no empty fields, and the murderer/accomplice are now Shreya/Shonali (the customer had only suggested Arthaur; the first generation had chosen Arthaur/Sina). Reviewer: 11 findings, all verified real and fixed by exact-match edit with the point-form copies kept in step: three Round 2 scripts naming the sculpture before the Round 3 evidence reveals it, an innocent (Camille) told to steer away from the murderer, a reversed-logic sentence, a self-contradictory time reference, a "my introduction" reference the introduction did not support, a grammar slip, and two they/them misgenders of the gender-neutral Quinn.

**Detect (shipped).** `package_completion_blocking_defects()` now reports `missing_round_content.<name>` for an empty `introduction`, or an empty `secret` with no `secrets`. Applied live by exact-text replacement on the live definition; migration `20261006150000_missing_introduction_or_secret_blocks_completion.sql`. Corpus check first (157 paid packages, 1,896 characters): only the three "TEST Mystery" fixture rows match. Rolled-back positive test returned `{missing_round_content.William}`.

**Not done / decisions.**
- The full-cast manual read was not done on the regenerated package; the reviewer pass, the gate, the detectors and a targeted check of the innocents against the murderer were. For that reason no `package_review_sweeps` row was inserted (it would inflate the recall figure).
- The reviewer's first-generation miss (William) is now covered by the gate; no `package_review_misses` row, since that package no longer exists.
- Open: the same Child run produced the William gap, so a Child that silently drops Round 1 fields may recur; the new gate check will hold and heal it.

**Key files.** `supabase/functions/_shared/rosterExtraction.ts`, `supabase/functions/mystery-webhook-trigger/index.ts`, `scripts/__tests__/conceptSnapshot.test.mjs`, `supabase/migrations/20261006150000_missing_introduction_or_secret_blocks_completion.sql`.
